### [84] [BaLoRA: Bayesian Low-Rank Adaptation of Large Scale Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08110)
*Dario Coscia,Sindy Löwe,Max Welling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BaLoRA是一种贝叶斯扩展的低秩适配方法，通过输入自适应噪声注入实现不确定性量化，并在保持计算效率的同时缩小了与全参数微调的精度差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA的低秩点估计更新限制表达能力，与全参数微调存在精度差距，且缺乏不确定性量化能力，限制了在可靠性关键场景中的应用。

Method: 提出BaLoRA，对LoRA矩阵进行贝叶斯扩展，采用新颖的输入自适应贝叶斯参数化，仅增加极少量参数和计算量。

Result: 在自然语言推理和视觉任务上缩小了与全参数微调的精度差距；在MOF带隙预测中，零样本测试时不确定性估计与模型误差的相关性优于LoRA集成，且随计算量单调提升。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯扩展不仅提供了良好校准的不确定性估计，其自适应噪声注入还显著提升了预测精度，在不牺牲准确率的前提下实现了不确定性量化与性能的双重改进。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) has become the standard for fine-tuning large pre-trained models at reduced computational cost. However, its low-rank point-estimate updates limit expressiveness, leave a persistent gap relative to full fine-tuning accuracy, and provide no built-in uncertainty quantification, limiting its applicability in settings where reliability matters as much as accuracy. We introduce BaLoRA, a Bayesian extension of LoRA with a novel input-adaptive Bayesian parameterization of LoRA matrices that adds minimal parameters and compute. Surprisingly, not only does the Bayesian extension yield well-calibrated uncertainty estimates, but the adaptive noise injection underlying our approach also significantly improves prediction accuracy, narrowing the gap with full fine-tuning across both natural language reasoning and vision tasks. When applied to band gap prediction in metal-organic frameworks, BaLoRA produces zero-shot test-time uncertainty estimates that correlate more strongly with model error than a trained ensemble of LoRA models, and improve monotonically with compute without sacrificing accuracy.

</details>


### [85] [TTCD:Transformer Integrated Temporal Causal Discovery from Non-Stationary Time Series Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08111)
*Omar Faruque,Sahara Ali,Xue Zheng,Jianwu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了TTCD框架，一种基于Transformer的端到端时间序列因果发现方法，通过非平稳特征学习器、重建引导的因果信号蒸馏和因果结构学习器，在非平稳、非线性和噪声环境下高效发现同期和滞后因果关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列因果发现方法在处理非平稳、非线性和噪声数据时存在局限性：约束性方法依赖条件独立性检验，在小样本和复杂分布下性能退化；基于评分的方法需要强统计假设；而专门处理变化点检测或分布偏移的方法缺乏统一解决方案。

Method: TTCD框架包含三个核心组件：1) 非平稳特征学习器，融合时域和频域注意力机制与动态非平稳性画像；2) 重建引导的因果信号蒸馏，通过Transformer解码器的重建过程去除噪声和虚假相关；3) 因果结构学习器，在蒸馏后的重建信号上推断因果图，无需对噪声分布或数据生成过程做限制性假设。

Result: 在合成数据、基准数据集和真实世界数据集上的实验表明，TTCD在准确性和与领域知识的一致性方面均持续优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: TTCD框架为复杂真实世界场景下的时间序列因果发现提供了一种有效的统一解决方案。

Abstract: The widespread availability of complex time series data in various domains such as environmental science, epidemiology, and economics demands robust causal discovery methods that can identify intricate contemporaneous and lagged relationships in non-stationary, nonlinear, and noisy settings. Existing constraint-based methods often rely heavily on conditional independence tests that degrade for limited data samples and complex distributions, while score-based methods impose strong statistical assumptions. Recent methods address special cases such as change point detection or distribution shifts, but struggle to provide a unified solution. We propose the Transformer Integrated Temporal Causal Discovery (TTCD) Framework, a novel end-to-end approach that learns contemporaneous and lagged causal relations from non-stationary time series. TTCD introduces a Non-Stationary Feature Learner integrating temporal and frequency-domain attention with dynamic non-stationarity profiling, and a custom Causal Structure Learner. A key innovation is reconstruction-guided causal signal distillation, to distill essential causal signals through the reconstruction process of the transformer decoder, which mitigates noise and spurious correlations while preserving meaningful dependencies. The Causal Structure Learner operates on distilled reconstructed signals to infer the underlying causal graph without restrictive assumptions on noise distributions or data generation processes. Experiments on synthetic, benchmark, and real world datasets show that TTCD consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in both accuracy and consistency with domain knowledge, demonstrating the approach's effectiveness for causal discovery in challenging real world contexts.

</details>


### [86] [Do Foundation Model Embeddings Improve Cross-Country Crop Yield Generalisation? A Leave-One-Country-Out Evaluation in Sub-Saharan Africa](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08113)
*Yaw Osei Adjei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了地理空间基础模型（Prithvi-EO-1.0-100M和ViT-Base）在跨国家小农玉米产量预测中的泛化能力，发现在留一国家出的验证方案下所有特征集均表现不佳（R²为负），揭示了一个显著的泛化差距，其根本限制在于国家间产量分布偏移而非表征质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 撒哈拉以南非洲的粮食安全规划需要跨国界的小农玉米产量准确预测，但现有基准大多仅报告国内性能，高估了模型真正的泛化能力。本文旨在严格评估地理空间基础模型在跨国家场景下的实际泛化性能。

Method: 采用留一国家出（Leave-One-Country-Out）交叉验证方案，在五个非洲国家的6,404块玉米田观测数据上，比较Prithvi-EO-1.0-100M和ViT-Base的嵌入特征与传统Sentinel-2光谱特征的预测性能。

Result: 国内随机交叉验证可获得中等R²值，但所有特征集在跨国家测试下表现均很差，普遍出现负R²；冻结的Prithvi-EO嵌入相比传统工程化光谱特征并无显著优势。

Conclusion: 跨国家产量预测的主要限制在于国家间产量分布偏移而非模型表征质量；本文发布了一个可复现的负面基准，供后续研究参考。

Abstract: Accurate predictions of smallholder maize yields across national boundaries are critical for food security planning in sub-Saharan Africa, yet most published benchmarks report within-country performance that overstates true generalisability. This paper evaluates whether geospatial foundation model embeddings, specifically Prithvi-EO-1.0-100M and ViT-Base, outperform traditional Sentinel-2 spectral features under a Leave-One-Country-Out cross-validation scheme on 6,404 maize field observations from five African countries. The results show a clear generalisability gap: within-country random cross-validation yields moderate R^2 values, but all feature sets perform poorly under cross-country testing, with universally negative R^2. Frozen Prithvi-EO embeddings provide no meaningful advantage over engineered spectral features for cross-country prediction in this setting. The paper argues that the main limitation is a shift in yield distribution between countries rather than representation quality and releases a reproducible negative benchmark for future work.

</details>


### [87] [Statistical Inference and Quality Measures of KV Cache Quantisations Inspired by TurboQuant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08114)
*Paolo D'Alberto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在固定比特预算下系统比较了三种KV缓存量化方案（KV、KQV、QKQV），发现KQV在n=4（实际主导预算）时全面最优；揭示了K-V非对称性（QKQV始终劣于KQV）；发现了与预算相关的性能交叉现象，并从Jensen机制角度解释了这一交叉的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在固定比特预算下公平比较三种KV缓存量化方案，揭示其统计与信息论特性差异，特别是解释QKQV与KQV之间性能交叉现象的深层机制。

Method: 从超球面Beta分布出发，理论推导了QJL对K向量内积方差的放大效应（π/2因子），并通过softmax的Jensen不等式进行非线性放大分析；实验上使用KL散度、几何K误差和6D距离三个指标，在不同分布、秩和预算（n=2~6）下进行系统比较。

Result: (1) n=4时KQV在所有指标上全面最优；(2) K-V非对称性无条件成立，QKQV在KL散度上始终劣于KQV；(3) 存在预算依赖的交叉：n∈{2,3,5}时QKQV几何K重构更优，n∈{4,6}时KQV更优。

Conclusion: n=4时QKQV的高K误差和高KL散度强烈表明Jensen机制是性能交叉的操作性原因；当假设不成立时（n∈{2,3,5}），QKQV在几何上获胜。这为KV量化方案选择提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: We analyse three KV cache quantization schemes under a fair bit budget: \textbf{KV} (scalar MSE baseline), \textbf{KQV} (WHT + MSE on $K$; WHT + MSE + QJL on $V$), and \textbf{QKQV} (WHT + MSE + QJL on both). Starting from the Beta distribution on the hypersphere, we trace how QJL on $K$ inflates inner product variance by $π/2$, which softmax amplifies nonlinearly via Jensen's inequality, and we present statistical inference and information metrics to highlight practical differences.
  Three empirical findings emerge. (1)~At $n=4$ (the practically dominant budget), KQV wins on every measure -- KL divergence, geometric $K$ error, and 6D distance -- across all distributions and ranks tested. (2)~The K--V asymmetry is unconditional: QKQV is consistently worse than KQV in KL divergence at every budget and distribution. (3)~A budget-dependent crossover exists: QKQV achieves better geometric $K$ reconstruction at $n \in \{2,3,5\}$, KQV at $n \in \{4,6\}$, invariant to rank and tail weight -- an open rate-distortion problem.
  $\mathrm{KL}(p_{\mathrm{ref}} \| p_{\mathrm{quant}})$, K-only by construction, bridges K direction error to routing corruption and output collapse. We present a sufficient condition when the Jensen mechanism amplifies superlinearly through the softmax. At $n \in \{2,3,5\}$, QKQV wins geometrically because this assumption does not bind. At $n=4$, elevated K error and KL divergence for QKQV strongly suggest the Jensen mechanism is the operative cause of the crossover, providing a new perspective and explanation.

</details>


### [88] [The Safety-Aware Denoiser for Text Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08116)
*Amman Yusuf,Zhejun Jiang,Mijung Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SAD（安全感知去噪器），一种用于文本扩散模型的安全引导框架，通过在迭代去噪过程中将文本样本引导至可证明安全的区域，在不重新训练模型的情况下实现安全约束，实验表明该方法在保持生成质量的同时显著降低了不安全内容生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本扩散模型的安全控制研究不足，而传统针对自回归模型的安全方法（如后处理过滤、推理时干预）无法有效应对文本扩散模型的安全风险。

Method: 提出SAD框架，在扩散模型的迭代去噪过程中修改生成方向，将最终采样文本引导至可证明安全的文本空间区域；该方法为推理时机制，避免昂贵的模型重新训练，实现灵活轻量的安全引导。

Result: 在危害分类、记忆泄漏、越狱攻击等安全评估维度上，SAD显著减少了不安全文本生成，同时保持了生成质量、多样性和流畅性，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 去噪过程中的安全引导为文本扩散模型提供了一种有效且可扩展的安全约束机制。

Abstract: Recent work on text diffusion models offers a promising alternative to autoregressive generation, but controlling their safety remains underexplored. Existing safety approaches are geared toward autoregressive models and typically rely on post-hoc filtering or inference-time interventions. These are inadequate for effectively addressing safety risks in text diffusion models. We propose the Safety-Aware Denoiser (SAD), a safety-guidance framework in text diffusion models. The SAD modifies the iterative denoising process such that the text sample at the final denoising step is steered toward provably safe regions of the text space. This inference-time method can integrate safety constraints into the denoiser, avoiding computationally expensive retraining of the underlying diffusion model and enabling flexible, lightweight safety guidance. We evaluate the safety of the generated text using the SAD, with respect to hazard taxonomy, memorization, and jailbreak. Experimental results show that SAD substantially reduces unsafe generations while preserving generation quality, diversity, and fluency, outperforming existing methods. These results demonstrate that our safety guidance during denoising provides an effective and scalable mechanism for enforcing safety in text diffusion models.

</details>


### [89] [Feature Repulsion and Spectral Lock-in: An Empirical Study of Two-Layer Network Grokking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08119)
*Yongzhong Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文验证了Tian (2025)的排斥定理在模加法任务中成立，但发现特征排斥的结构（B的符号）与参数更新的谱特征存在分离：平方激活下谱呈现秩-2结构且可检测，而ReLU下保持秩-1且无法检测，表明排斥效应的可观测性关键依赖于激活函数的导数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tian (2025)的排斥定理（Theorem 6）从理论上预测了grokking过程中相似特征之间存在排斥力，但该定理未指明这一机制何时在经验上可观测，以及是否在参数更新中留下可测量的谱特征。本文旨在通过实验填补这一空白。

Method: 在Tian的模加法实验设置（M=71, K=2048, MSE损失）上，分别使用σ=x²和σ=ReLU激活函数，检验B矩阵的符号规则在top-200最相似特征对上的符合率，并通过滚动特征间隙σ₂/σ₃检测器监测参数更新ΔW的谱特征。

Result: 符号规则在两种激活下均高度成立（x²上从0.865升至0.985，ReLU上饱和至1.000）。但谱特征强烈依赖激活函数：x²下检测器在15/15个grokking种子中于第174轮触发（0/15非grokking对照），谱为秩-2；ReLU下检测器从未触发，谱保持秩-1。

Conclusion: 特征排斥的结构机制与参数更新的谱可观测性之间存在分离：B的符号仅依赖于特征内积矩阵，但排斥效应如何转化为权重更新取决于激活函数的导数σ'。这呼应了Tian的Theorem 5中聚焦式（幂律）与扩散式（ReLU）记忆化的区分。

Abstract: Tian (2025) proves a repulsion theorem (Theorem 6) for the matrix $ B = (\widetilde{F}^\top \widetilde{F} + ηI)^{-1} $ during the interactive feature-learning stage of grokking: similar features have negative off-diagonal entries $ B_{j\ell} $, producing an effective repulsive force that drives them apart. However, the theorem does not specify when this mechanism becomes empirically observable, nor whether it leaves a measurable spectral signature in the parameter updates. We test this directly on Tian's modular addition setup ($ M = 71 $, $ K = 2048 $, MSE loss) and observe a clear structure-mechanism dissociation. The predicted sign rule holds robustly on the top-200 most-similar feature pairs across activations (empirical sign-match rising from 0.865 to 0.985 on $ σ= x^2 $ across 5 seeds, and saturating at 1.000 on $ σ= \operatorname{ReLU} $). However, the spectral signature in the parameter updates is strongly activation-dependent. With $ σ= x^2 $, a simple slope detector on the rolling eigengap $ σ_2 / σ_3 $ of $ ΔW $ fires in 15/15 grokking seeds at epoch 174 (IQR [173,174]) and in 0/15 non-grokking controls, with 229$ \times $ late-stage magnitude separation; the spectrum is rank-2. In contrast, with $ σ= \operatorname{ReLU} $, the detector never fires and the spectrum remains effectively rank-1. This dissociation aligns with Tian's Theorem 5 distinction between focused (power-law) and spreading (ReLU) memorization: while the sign structure of $ B $ depends only on $ \widetilde{F}^\top \widetilde{F} $, how feature repulsion translates into weight updates critically depends on the activation derivative $ σ' $.

</details>


### [90] [Towards Customized Multimodal Role-Play](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08129)
*Chao Tang,Jianzong Wu,Qingyu Shi,Ye Tian,Aixi Zhang,Hao Jiang,Jiangning Zhang,Yunhai Tong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多模态角色扮演任务CMRP，构建RoleScape-20数据集，并设计UniCharacter两阶段训练框架（Unified-SFT + Character-GRPO），仅需10张图片和对应交互样例即可实现角色个性、对话风格和视觉形象的一致性定制，在100 GPU小时内完成训练，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态理解和生成模型虽能实现人机交互，但在联合定制角色的个性（persona）、对话风格和视觉身份并保持跨模态输出一致性方面尚属空白，缺乏相关任务定义、数据集和方法。

Method: 提出UniCharacter两阶段训练框架：第一阶段为统一监督微调（Unified-SFT）进行多模态对齐；第二阶段为角色特定群体相对策略优化（Character-GRPO）实现个性化定制。仅需10张图片和对应交互样例，约100 GPU小时完成训练。

Result: 在RoleScape-20数据集上，所提方法大幅优于此前方法。消融实验验证了跨模态一致性设计和少样本定制策略的有效性。

Conclusion: CMRP任务结合统一建模为下一代富有角色个性与沉浸感的交互智能体提供了基础。

Abstract: Unified multimodal understanding and generation models enable richer human-AI interaction. Yet jointly customizing a character's persona, dialogue style, and visual identity while maintaining output consistency across modalities remains largely unexplored. To mitigate this gap, we introduce a new task, Customized Multimodal Role-Play (CMRP). We construct the RoleScape-20 dataset comprising 20 characters, including training and evaluation data that cover persona, stylistic descriptions, visual/expressive cues, and text-image interactions. Building on a unified model, we devise UniCharacter, a two-stage training framework containing Unified Supervised Finetuning (Unified-SFT) and character-specific group relative policy optimization (Character-GRPO). Given only 10 images plus corresponding interaction examples, the model acquires the target character and exhibits coherent persona, style, and visual identity in both generated text and images. This process takes about 100 GPU hours. Experiments on the RoleScape-20 dataset show that the proposed method substantially outperforms prior approaches. Ablation studies further validate the effectiveness of our cross-modal consistency design and few-shot customization strategy. We argue that CMRP, coupled with unified modeling, provides a basis for next-generation characterful and immersive interactive agents.

</details>


### [91] [Additive Atomic Forests for Symbolic Function and Antiderivative Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08130)
*Reda Belaiche*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种从数据中同时符号恢复函数及其原函数的框架，核心包括导数代数（通过乘积法则和链式法则自扩展的函数-导数对库）、两种互补原语（EML和SOL）以及加性原子森林（通过连续优化或穷举搜索拟合数据），无需符号积分即可同时获得函数F及其导数F'。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有符号回归方法通常需要分别处理函数和其原函数（或导数），且缺乏一种能同时恢复两者、无需符号积分步骤的统一框架。作者希望构建一个能自动扩展、自构建的函数库，实现从数据中联合恢复函数及其原函数。

Method: 1) 导数代数：从种子初等函数集出发，通过乘积法则和链式法则递归生成函数-导数对的自扩展库；2) 两种原语：EML (e^u - ln v) 保证理论完备性，SOL (sin u - cos v) 使三角原子在深度1可用；3) 加性原子森林：通过连续优化或穷举搜索，将原始树的和（可选乘性组合）拟合数据，利用预制导数同时编码F和F'。

Result: 在17个分类基准测试中，稀疏原子组合在13个数据集上达到或超越XGBoost的性能，同时生成可解释的公式。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种无需符号积分即可同时恢复函数及其原函数的有效方法，库的自构建特性使其表达能力随库增大而增强，实验结果验证了其在可解释性和性能上的优势。

Abstract: We present a framework for the simultaneous symbolic recovery of a function and its antiderivative from data. The framework rests on three ideas. First, a derivative algebra: the observation that the product rule $\frac{d}{dx}[f \cdot g] = f'g + fg'$ and the chain rule, applied to a seed set of elementary functions, generate a self-expanding system of function-derivative pairs -- a living library that grows each time a new function is discovered. Second, two complementary primitives -- EML$\,(e^u - \ln v)$, which is theoretically complete for all elementary functions, and SOL$\,(\sin u - \cos v)$, introduced here, which makes trigonometric atoms available at depth~1 instead of depth~$\sim$8 -- that seed the library with core atoms cheaply. Third, additive atomic forests: finite sums of primitive trees, optionally composed via multiplicative nodes, whose derivatives are fitted to data by continuous optimisation or by exhaustive search over the library. Because differentiation of each atom is determined by construction, the forest simultaneously encodes a symbolic expression $F$ and its derivative $F'$; no symbolic integration step is required.
  The library is not a fixed object: it self-constructs from a small seed set by recursive application of the product rule, chain rule, and the two primitives, and it can grow as newly discovered functions are folded back in. The larger the library, the richer the expressible class of candidate functions. We give conditional completeness, additive-depth, and analytic simultaneous-recovery results for the framework. Empirically, in our reported runs on 17 classification benchmarks, sparse atom combinations match or exceed XGBoost on 13 datasets while producing interpretable formulas.

</details>


### [92] [Interactive Inverse Reinforcement Learning of Interaction Scenarios via Bi-level Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08131)
*Yue Mao,Shicheng Liu,Siyuan Xu,Minghui Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种交互式逆强化学习（IIRL）框架，将其形式化为随机双层优化问题，并开发了BISIRL算法，该算法通过内外双循环分别求解下层的奖励函数学习问题和上层的交互策略学习问题，理论证明收敛性并通过实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统IRL中学习器只能被动观察专家演示，无法应用于需要主动与专家交互的场景。为弥补这一差距，本文研究交互式IRL（IIRL），使学习器在与专家交互过程中主动推断专家的奖励函数。

Method: 将IIRL形式化为随机双层优化问题：下层学习奖励函数以解释专家行为，上层学习与专家交互的策略。提出双循环算法BISIRL，内循环求解下层问题，外循环求解上层问题。

Result: 从理论上证明了BISIRL算法的收敛性，并通过大量实验验证了算法的有效性。

Conclusion: BISIRL成功解决了交互式场景下的逆强化学习问题，为主动交互式奖励函数学习提供了理论保证和有效算法。

Abstract: Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) learns a reward function and a corresponding policy that best fit the demonstration data of an expert. However, in the current IRL setting, the learner is isolated from the expert and can only passively observe the expert demonstrations. This limits the applicability of IRL to interactive settings, where the learner actively interacts with the expert and needs to infer the expert's reward function from the interactions. To bridge the gap, this paper studies interactive IRL (IIRL) where a learner aims to learn the reward function of an expert and a policy to interact with the expert during its interactions with the expert. We formulate IIRL as a stochastic bi-level optimization problem where the lower level learns a reward function to explain the behaviors of the expert, and the upper level learns a policy to interact with the expert. We develop a double-loop algorithm, Bi-level Interactive Scenarios Inverse Reinforcement Learning (BISIRL), which solves the lower-level problem in the inner loop and the upper-level problem in the outer loop. We formally guarantee that BISIRL converges and validate our algorithm through extensive experiments.

</details>


### [93] [DARE: Diffusion Language Model Activation Reuse for Efficient Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08134)
*Natalia Frumkin,Bokun Wang,Hung-Yueh Chiang,Chi-Chih Chang,Mohamed S. Abdelfattah,Diana Marculescu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DARE通过利用扩散大语言模型中自注意力的token级冗余，实现KV缓存和输出激活的重用，在不显著降低生成质量的前提下提升推理效率，最高可达1.20倍每层延迟降低和87%注意力激活重用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型虽具并行生成和更快推理潜力，但开源dLLM在效率和质量上仍落后于自回归模型，亟需提升其计算效率。

Method: DARE包含两个互补机制：DARE-KV重用缓存的键值激活，DARE-O重用输出激活，通过识别双向自注意力中token之间的高度相关性来减少冗余计算。

Result: 每层延迟降低最高1.20倍，注意力激活重用率最高87%，推理和代码生成基准性能下降极小（DARE-KV平均2.0%，DARE-O平均1.2%）。

Conclusion: Token级激活重用是提升扩散LLM效率的有效策略，且与prefix caching和Fast-dLLM等技术兼容，无需重新训练即可获得叠加收益。

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs) have emerged as a promising alternative to auto-regressive (AR) models, offering greater expressive capacity and potential for parallel generation and faster inference. However, open-source dLLMs remain immature, lagging behind AR models in both efficiency and quality.
  We identify an underexplored property of dLLMs: *token-wise redundancy* in bi-directional self-attention. Self-attention activations are highly correlated across tokens, and temporal changes in query representations can predict redundancy in corresponding key, value, and output activations.
  We introduce DARE, with two complementary mechanisms: DARE-KV, which reuses cached key-value (KV) activations, and DARE-O, which reuses output activations to reduce redundant computation while preserving quality.
  DARE achieves up to 1.20x per-layer latency reduction and reuses up to 87% of attention activations, with negligible degradation on reasoning and code-generation benchmarks. DARE-KV and DARE-O incur average performance drops of only 2.0% and 1.2%, respectively. Combined with techniques such as prefix caching and Fast-dLLM, DARE provides additive gains without retraining.
  These results establish token-wise reuse as an effective strategy for improving the efficiency of diffusion-based LLMs while preserving generation fidelity. Code: https://github.com/enyac-group/DARE

</details>


### [94] [Weight Pruning Amplifies Bias: A Multi-Method Study of Compressed LLMs for Edge AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08137)
*Plawan Kumar Rath,Rahul Maliakkal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了三种指令微调模型在四种剪枝方法、四个稀疏度水平下的公平性影响，发现激活感知剪枝(Wanda)虽然能保持困惑度，却导致最大的偏见放大（刻板印象依赖得分增加83.7%，47-59%的无偏见样本产生新刻板行为）；随机剪枝虽破坏语言能力但偏见仅随机水平；非结构化剪枝在真实边缘硬件上零存储节省和零推理延迟降低；剪枝导致偏见状态翻转率(47-59%)是量化的近三倍，表明困惑度评估无法保证行为等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 剪枝被广泛用于在资源受限的IoT和边缘设备上部署大语言模型，但其对模型公平性的影响尚不明确，现有研究过度依赖困惑度评估而忽视了公平性维度。

Method: 对三种指令微调模型(Gemma-2-9b-it, Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3, Phi-3.5-mini-instruct)，采用三种剪枝方法(Random, Magnitude, Wanda)在10-70%四个稀疏度水平下，使用5个随机种子在12,148个BBQ偏差基准样本上进行控制实验，共产生2,368,860条推理记录。

Result: Wanda剪枝在50%稀疏度下困惑度仅增加3.5%，但70%稀疏度下刻板印象依赖得分增加83.7%，47-59%先前无偏样本产生新刻板行为；随机剪枝导致困惑度超过10^4甚至10^8但偏见仅随机水平；非结构化剪枝在边缘硬件上零存储节省和零延迟降低；180次比较中78.3%显著(p<0.05)，平均|h|=0.305；剪枝偏见解耦翻转率(47-59%)是量化研究(21%)的近三倍。

Conclusion: 困惑度评估无法保证行为等价性，剪枝对对齐造成的风险远超量化；IoT部署流水线需在部署剪枝模型前进行偏见感知验证。

Abstract: Weight pruning is widely advocated for deploying Large Language Models on resource-constrained IoT and edge devices, yet its impact on model fairness remains poorly understood. We conduct a controlled empirical study of three instruction-tuned models (Gemma-2-9b-it, Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3, Phi-3.5-mini-instruct) across three pruning methods (Random, Magnitude, Wanda) at four sparsity levels (10-70%) on 12,148 BBQ bias benchmark items with 5 random seeds, totaling 2,368,860 inference records. Our results reveal a Smart Pruning Paradox: activation-aware pruning (Wanda) preserves perplexity nearly perfectly (just 3.5% increase at 50% sparsity for Mistral-7B), yet produces the highest bias amplification, with Stereotype Reliance Score increasing 83.7% and 47-59% of previously unbiased items developing new stereotypical behaviors at 70% sparsity. Random pruning destroys language capability entirely (perplexity exceeding $10^4$ and reaching $10^8$) but produces only random-chance bias. We further show that unstructured pruning provides zero storage savings and zero inference latency reduction on real edge hardware, undermining the primary motivation for its use in IoT deployment. Of 180 dense-vs-pruned comparisons, 141 (78.3%) are significant ($p < 0.05$) with mean $|h| = 0.305$. Published quantization studies report up to 21% of responses flipping between biased and unbiased states; our pruning results show transition rates nearly three times higher (47-59%), suggesting pruning poses a categorically greater risk to alignment than quantization. These findings demonstrate that perplexity-based evaluation provides false assurance of behavioral equivalence, and that IoT deployment pipelines require bias-aware validation before deploying pruned models at the edge.

</details>


### [95] [DataArc-SynData-Toolkit: A Unified Closed-Loop Framework for Multi-Path, Multimodal, and Multilingual Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08138)
*Zhichao Shi,Cehao Yang,Hao Zhou,Xiaojun Wu,Huajie Li,Xuhui Jiang,Chengjin Xu,Yuanzhuo Wang,Jian Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DataArc-SynData-Toolkit，一个开源、配置驱动的端到端合成数据生成框架，具有可视化界面、统一的质量可控合成范式和高度模块化架构，在多个应用场景中实现了生成效率与数据质量的最优平衡，显著降低了合成数据生成的技术门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成数据工具存在工作流复杂、数据标准碎片化、跨模态可扩展性有限等问题，严重阻碍了其在大语言模型（LLMs）领域的广泛应用，尤其是在专业领域和低资源语言场景下。

Method: 开发了DataArc-SynData-Toolkit开源框架，包含三个核心设计：(1) 配置驱动的端到端流水线，配备直观的可视化界面和简化CLI；(2) 统一的质量可控合成范式，标准化多源数据生成；(3) 高度模块化架构，支持无缝的多模态、多语言和多任务扩展。

Result: 在多个应用场景中的实验结果表明，该工具包在生成效率与数据质量之间达到了最优平衡。

Conclusion: 通过提供端到端和可视交互的流水线，DataArc-SynData-Toolkit显著降低了合成数据生成及后续模型训练的技术障碍，加速了其在真实世界应用中的实际部署。

Abstract: Synthetic data has emerged as a crucial solution to the data scarcity bottleneck in large language models (LLMs), particularly for specialized domains and low-resource languages. However, the broader adoption of existing synthetic data tools is severely hindered by convoluted workflows, fragmented data standards, and limited scalability across modalities. To address these limitations, we develop DataArc-SynData-Toolkit, an open-source framework featuring: (1) a configuration-driven, end-to-end pipeline equipped with an intuitive visual interface and simplified CLI for exceptional usability; (2) a unified, quality-controllable synthesis paradigm that standardizes multi-source data generation to ensure high reusability; and (3) a highly modular architecture designed for seamless multimodal, multilingual, and multi-task adaptation. We apply the toolkit in multiple application scenarios. Experimental results demonstrate that our toolkit achieves an optimal balance between generation efficiency and data quality. By offering an end-to-end and visually interactive pipeline, DataArc-SynData-Toolkit significantly lowers the technical barrier to synthetic data generation and subsequent model training, accelerating its practical deployment in real-world applications.

</details>


### [96] [Reasoning emerges from constrained inference manifolds in large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08142)
*Yanbiao Ma,Fei Luo,Linfeng Zhang,Chuangxin Zhao,Mingxuan Wang,Yinan Wu,Zhe Qian,Yang Lu,Long Chen,Zhao Cao,Xiaoshuai Hao,Ji-Rong Wen,Jungong Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过分析大语言模型推理过程中的内部表征演化，发现推理动态会自组织成低维流形，并提出了一个基于几何和信息约束的无标签诊断方法，用于评估推理质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试将任务性能与内部推理质量混为一谈，无法真正衡量推理过程的本质。作者希望从内在动力学角度研究推理，而非依赖标注基准。

Method: 研究推理过程中内部表征的演化动态，分析其几何结构（低维流形嵌入高维空间），并识别有效推理的三个条件：充分的表征表达能力、自发的流形压缩、以及压缩子空间中非退化信息体积的保持。

Result: 发现推理动态普遍存在几何压缩现象，但仅有压缩不足以保证稳定可靠的推理；有效推理需要三个条件同时满足，否则会出现病态推理动态；基于此提出了一个仅从内部动态计算的无标签诊断方法。

Conclusion: LLM中的推理本质上受几何和信息约束的支配，为基准中心评估范式提供了补充框架。

Abstract: Reasoning in large language models is predominantly evaluated through labeled benchmarks, conflating task performance with the quality of internal inference. Here we study reasoning as an intrinsic dynamical process by examining the evolution of internal representations during inference. We find that inference-time dynamics consistently self-organize into low-dimensional manifolds embedded within high-dimensional representation spaces. we find that such geometric compression, although pervasive, is not sufficient for stable or reliable reasoning. Instead, effective reasoning dynamics emerge within a constrained structural regime characterized by three conditions: adequate representational expressivity, spontaneous manifold compression, and preservation of non-degenerate information volume within the compressed subspace. Models outside this regime exhibit characteristic pathological inference dynamics. Based on these insights, we introduce a unified, label-free diagnostic computed solely from internal dynamics. These findings suggest that reasoning in LLMs is fundamentally governed by geometric and informational constraints, offering a complementary framework to benchmark-centric assessment.

</details>


### [97] [Retrieval Mechanisms Surpass Long-Context Scaling in Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08217)
*Rishi Ahuja,Kumar Prateek,Simranjit Singh,Vijay Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）从NLP继承了“长上下文提升预测质量”的假设，但实验表明该假设不成立：更长的历史窗口反而导致预测误差上升（逆缩放定律），而基于检索增强的RAFT模型通过选择性检索实现了更优性能（MSE 0.379），证明未来基础模型应转向选择性检索架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验时间序列基础模型中“输入更多历史数据可提升预测质量”这一从NLP继承的假设是否成立。

Method: 在ETTh1基准上运行连续上下文架构（含PatchTST），对比不同上下文窗口长度的预测表现，并评估检索增强预测（RAFT）作为替代方案。

Result: 逆缩放定律：上下文越长预测误差越大（3000步窗口导致性能下降68%以上）；RAFT以720步固定窗口和选择性检索取得MSE 0.379，优于长上下文配置和零样本基础模型（Chronos、Moirai），且计算成本更低。

Conclusion: 注意力机制难以忽略历史无关波动，未来基础模型应在架构上转向选择性检索机制。

Abstract: Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) have borrowed the long context paradigm from natural language processing under the premise that feeding more history into the model improves forecast quality. But in stochastic domains, distant history is often just high-frequency noise, not signal. Hence, the proposed work tests whether this premise actually holds by running continuous context architectures (PatchTST included) through the ETTh1 benchmark. The obtained results contradict the premise: an inverse scaling law shows up clearly, with forecasting error rising as context gets longer. A 3,000-step window causes performance to drop by over 68%, evidence that attention mechanisms are poor at ignoring irrelevant historical volatility. Retrieval-Augmented Forecasting (RAFT) is evaluated as an alternative. RAFT achieves a mean squared error (MSE) of 0.379 with a fixed 720-step window and selective retrieval, outperforming both long-context configurations and zero-shot foundation models (Chronos, Moirai) despite requiring far less computation. In addition, the retrieval step injects only the most relevant historical segments as dynamic exogenous variables, which gives the model a context-informed inductive bias it cannot build on its own from raw sequences. Therefore, foundation models going forward need to shift architecturally toward selective retrieval.

</details>


### [98] [NoiseRater: Meta-Learned Noise Valuation for Diffusion Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08144)
*Fang Wu,Haokai Zhao,Da Xing,Hanqun Cao,Tinson Xu,Yanchao Li,Xiangru Tang,Zehong Wang,Aaron Tu,Kuan Pang,Hanchen Wang,Hongbin Lin,Zeqi Zhou,Yinxi Li,Peng Xia,Li Erran Li,Molei Tao,Jure Leskovec,Aditya Joshi,Yejin Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出NoiseRater框架，通过元学习对扩散模型训练中的噪声样本进行重要性评估与自适应加权，证明噪声并非同等重要，优先选择信息量大的噪声可提升训练效率和生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型训练中通常将所有注入噪声视为同等信息量，本文挑战这一假设，探索噪声样本的价值差异及自适应加权对训练的影响。

Method: 设计参数化噪声评分器，根据数据和时间步对单个噪声实例分配重要性分数；通过双层优化在内部循环扩散更新后提升下游验证性能来训练评分器；采用解耦的两阶段流水线，从元训练时的软加权过渡到标准训练时的硬噪声选择。

Result: 在FFHQ和ImageNet上的实验表明噪声样本贡献不均，优先选择信息丰富的噪声能同时提升训练效率和生成质量。

Conclusion: 噪声价值评估是改进扩散模型训练的一个被忽视的新维度。

Abstract: Diffusion models have achieved remarkable success across a wide range of generative tasks, yet their training paradigm largely treats injected noise as uniformly informative. In this work, we challenge this assumption and introduce NoiseRater, a meta-learning framework for instance-level noise valuation in diffusion model training. We propose a parametric noise rater that assigns importance scores to individual noise realizations conditioned on data and timestep, enabling adaptive reweighting of the training objective. The rater is trained via bilevel optimization to improve downstream validation performance after inner-loop diffusion updates. To enable efficient deployment, we further design a decoupled two-stage pipeline that transitions from soft weighting during meta-training to hard noise selection during standard training. Extensive experiments on FFHQ and ImageNet demonstrate that not all noise samples contribute equally, and that prioritizing informative noise improves both training efficiency and generation quality. Our results establish noise valuation as a complementary and previously underexplored axis for improving diffusion model training. Our code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/NoiseRater-DEB116.

</details>


### [99] [Feature Rivalry in Sparse Autoencoder Representations: A Mechanistic Study of Uncertainty-Driven Feature Competition in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08149)
*Harshavardhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"特征竞争"(Feature Rivalry)概念——稀疏自编码器中负相关的特征对，并证明其是模型不确定性的机制性标志。在Gemma-2-2B上，高熵问题在特定层产生更强竞争，且沿竞争方向进行激活操控可改变模型输出，基于竞争分数的预测达到AUROC=0.689。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏自编码器(SAE)虽能分解LLM表征为可解释特征，但这些特征在不确定性条件下的交互机制尚未被理解。

Method: 在Gemma-2-2B上使用Gemma Scope SAE，通过PopQA数据集按响应熵划分进行受控域内实验；采用激活操控(沿竞争方向vs随机方向)进行因果测试；基于活跃SAE特征解码器向量的成对余弦相似度构建逐提示竞争分数。

Result: 高熵问题在层0和层12产生显著更强的特征竞争(p=5.3x10^-26, p=5.8x10^-5)；15/20个竞争特征对在低操控强度下沿竞争方向比随机方向引起更多输出变化；竞争分数预测准确率AUROC=0.689(softmax置信度为0.808)。

Conclusion: 特征竞争是模型不确定性的机制性标志，可将不确定性定位到残差流的特定处理阶段，且竞争方向具有因果上游性；但基于竞争的预测尚未超越softmax置信度。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) decompose large language model representations into interpretable features, but how these features interact under uncertainty remains poorly understood. We introduce Feature Rivalry -- negatively correlated SAE feature pairs -- and study whether rivalry serves as a mechanistic signature of model uncertainty in Gemma-2-2B using Gemma Scope SAEs. Through a controlled within-domain experiment on PopQA split by response entropy, we find that high-entropy questions produce significantly stronger feature rivalry at layers 0 and 12 relative to low-entropy questions (p=5.3x10^-26 and p=5.8x10^-5 respectively), localizing uncertainty to specific processing stages in the residual stream. We then test whether rivalry is causally upstream of model outputs via activation steering along rivalry axes -- finding that steering along the rivalry direction (vec_A - vec_B) causes more output changes than random directions at low steering multipliers across 15 of 20 rival feature pairs. Finally, a per-prompt rivalry score derived from pairwise cosine similarities of active SAE feature decoder vectors predicts answer correctness (AUROC=0.689), approaching but not matching softmax confidence (AUROC=0.808).

</details>


### [100] [Temporal-Decay Shapley: A Time-Aware Data Valuation Framework for Time-Series Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08153)
*Chuwen Pang,Bing Mi,Kongyang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了三种增强的时间Shapley数据估值方法（TDS、改进TDS、MS-TDS），通过时间衰减机制和多尺度融合策略，解决了传统数据估值方法忽略时序数据中样本价值随时间变化的问题，在噪声检测和高价值数据识别任务中优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据估值方法假设样本独立同分布，忽略了时间序列数据中样本价值随时间动态变化的特性，导致对时序数据的估值不准确。

Method: 提出了三种渐进增强的时间Shapley方法：1) Temporal-Decay Shapley (TDS) 引入指数衰减权重融入时间信息；2) 改进TDS采用幂指数衰减以适应非线性时间漂移；3) Multi-Scale Temporal-Decay Shapley (MS-TDS) 构建多尺度融合机制，通过并行多尺度估值和样本级自适应融合，平衡短期热点样本和长期基础样本的价值。

Result: 所提方法在噪声检测和高价值数据识别任务中普遍优于传统方法，在强时间依赖性场景下优势更为明显，有效提升了数据估值的准确性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 时间衰减机制和多尺度融合策略能够有效捕捉时序数据中样本价值的动态变化，为时序数据的数据选择、噪声检测和模型优化提供了更准确的估值方法。

Abstract: With the rapid development of machine learning applications on time-series data, accurately assessing the value of training samples has become essential for data selection, noise detection, and model optimization. However, traditional data valuation methods usually assume that samples are independent and identically distributed, and thus ignore the time-varying nature of sample value in time-series data. This paper proposes an improved temporal Shapley data valuation method that enables accurate sample valuation for time-series data through a temporal decay mechanism and a multi-scale fusion strategy. Specifically, we propose three progressively enhanced temporal Shapley methods. Temporal-Decay Shapley (TDS) incorporates temporal information into Shapley value computation through exponential decay weights; the improved TDS adopts power exponential decay to better adapt to nonlinear temporal drift; and Multi-Scale Temporal-Decay Shapley (MS-TDS) constructs a multi-scale fusion mechanism that balances the value of short-term hotspot samples and long-term foundational samples through parallel multi-scale valuation and sample-level adaptive fusion. Experimental results show that the proposed methods generally outperform traditional methods in noise detection and high-value data identification tasks, with more evident advantages under most strongly temporal settings, thereby effectively improving the accuracy and robustness of data valuation.

</details>


### [101] [Quantitative Sobolev Approximation Bounds for Neural Operators with Empirical Validation on Burgers Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08170)
*Nicole Hao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了Sobolev空间中神经算子逼近的理论框架，证明了连续非线性算子可被神经算子以$\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-d/s})$参数在$H^t$范数下一致逼近，并通过一维粘性Burgers方程上的FNO实验验证了$H^1$误差与参数量的幂律关系$\|\mathcal{G}-\mathcal{G}_θ\|_{H^{1}} \approx C N^{-α}$，经验指数α≈1.4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子在无限维函数空间映射学习中表现出色，但其在Sobolev范数下的逼近性质尚未得到充分量化，而Sobolev范数同时控制函数值和导数，是PDE适定性、稳定性和泛化的自然度量。

Method: 1) 建立泛函分析框架，证明在输入受限$H^s(D)$紧子集条件下，连续非线性算子$\mathcal{G}: H^{s}(D)\to H^{t}(D')$可由参数量为$\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-d/s})$的神经算子在$H^t$范数下一致逼近；2) 以一维粘性Burgers方程解算子为基准，使用$H^1$损失训练FNO，系统扫描模型规模并测试Sobolev误差。

Result: FNO测试$H^1$误差低至$\mathcal{O}(10^{-7})$，相对误差约$10^{-3}$；Sobolev误差与参数量呈幂律关系$\|\mathcal{G}-\mathcal{G}_θ\|_{H^{1}} \approx C N^{-α}$，经验指数α≈1.4；大FNO训练出现优化不稳定性。

Conclusion: Sobolev空间逼近理论能有效预测神经算子的标度行为，为神经算子在PDE求解中的理论分析提供了定量依据。

Abstract: Neural operators have emerged as a powerful tool for learning mappings between infinite-dimensional function spaces. However, their approximation properties in Sobolev norms remain poorly quantified, even though these norms control both function values and derivatives and are the natural metrics for PDE well-posedness, stability, and generalization. We develop a functional-analytic framework for operator learning in Sobolev spaces and connect it to the numerical behavior of Fourier Neural Operators (FNOs) on a prototypical PDE. First, for a continuous nonlinear operator $\mathcal{G}: H^{s}(D)\to H^{t}(D')$ with $s > d/2$ and inputs restricted to a compact subset of $H^{s}(D)$, we prove that $\mathcal{G}$ can be uniformly approximated in $H^{t}$-norm by a neural operator with $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-d/s})$ trainable parameters. This yields an explicit complexity--error relation of the form $\|\mathcal{G}-\mathcal{G}_θ\|_{H^{t}} \lesssim C N^{-s/d}$. We then study the one-dimensional viscous Burgers solution operator $\mathcal{G}: u_{0}\mapsto u(\cdot,1)$ on a bounded $H^{1}$-ball and train FNOs with an $H^{1}$-loss. Across a sweep of model sizes, we obtain test $H^{1}$-errors down to $\mathcal{O}(10^{-7})$ and relative errors of order $10^{-3}$, with predictions accurately matching both solutions and spatial derivatives on held-out data. A log-log plot of Sobolev error versus parameter count exhibits an approximate power law $\|\mathcal{G}-\mathcal{G}_θ\|_{H^{1}} \approx C N^{-α}$ with empirical exponent $α\approx 1.4$, and long-horizon training reveals optimization instabilities in large FNOs, providing quantitative evidence that Sobolev-space approximation theory meaningfully predicts neural-operator scaling behavior.

</details>


### [102] [CERSA: Cumulative Energy-Retaining Subspace Adaptation for Memory-Efficient Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08174)
*Jingze Ge,Xue Geng,Yun Liu,Wanqi Dong,Wang Zhe Mark,Min Wu,Ngai-Man Cheung,Bharadwaj Veeravalli,Xulei Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CERSA方法，利用SVD保留90%-95%谱能量的主成分进行低秩微调，在降低内存消耗的同时，在图像识别、文生图、自然语言理解等多个任务上超越现有PEFT方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PEFT方法（如LoRA）采用低秩更新无法充分捕捉全参数微调中权重变化的秩特征，导致性能差距；同时仍需要大量内存存储完整冻结权重，在资源受限场景下效率有限。

Method: CERSA利用奇异值分解(SVD)保留占谱能量90%-95%的主成分，仅对从该主子空间导出的低秩表示进行微调，大幅降低内存消耗。

Result: 在图像识别、文生图、自然语言理解等多个领域和不同规模模型上的实验表明，CERSA在一致超越现有最优PEFT方法的同时，实现了更低的存储需求。

Conclusion: CERSA通过保留主要谱能量的主子空间进行低秩微调，有效弥补了PEFT方法的性能差距并显著降低内存开销，是一种高效的新型微调范式。

Abstract: To mitigate the memory constraints associated with fine-tuning large pre-trained models, existing parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods, such as LoRA, rely on low-rank updates. However, such updates fail to fully capture the rank characteristics of the weight modifications observed in full-parameter fine-tuning, resulting in a performance gap. Furthermore, LoRA and other existing PEFT methods still require substantial memory to store the full set of frozen weights, limiting their efficiency in resource-constrained settings. To addres these limitations, we introduce Cumulative Energy-Retaining Subspace Adaptation (CERSA), a novel fine-tuning paradigm that leverages singular value decomposition (SVD) to retain only the principal components responsible for 90% to 95% of the spectral energy. By fine-tuning low-rank representations derived from this principal subspace, CERSA significantly reduces memory consumption. We conduct extensive evaluations of CERSA across models of varying scales and domains, including image recognition, text-to-image generation, and natural language understanding. Empirical results demonstrate that CERSA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art PEFT methods while achieving substantially lower memory requirements. The code will be publicly released.

</details>


### [103] [Generalized Category Discovery in Federated Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08178)
*Zhongzheng Yuan,Lianshuai Guo,Xunkai Li,Wenyu Wang,Meixia Qu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GCD-FGL框架，解决联邦图学习中的广义类别发现问题，通过客户端拓扑可靠语义对齐发现和服务端层次原型对齐策略，在五个数据集上平均HRScore提升+4.86。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦图学习方法基于封闭世界假设，无法处理动态环境中新类别持续涌现的场景，因此需要研究联邦图广义类别发现问题（FGGCD）。

Method: 提出GCD-FGL框架：客户端侧采用拓扑可靠语义对齐与发现（Topology-Reliable Semantic Alignment and Discovery）缓解邻域吸收效应；服务端侧采用层次原型对齐（Hierarchical Prototype Alignment）解决全局语义不一致问题。

Result: 在五个真实图数据集上，GCD-FGL consistently优于现有基线方法，平均HRScore绝对增益达+4.86。

Conclusion: GCD-FGL有效解决了联邦图学习中的新类别发现与知识保留问题，为动态开放环境下的分布式图学习提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Federated Graph Learning (FGL) enables collaborative learning over distributed graph data, yet existing approaches largely rely on a closed-world assumption, limiting their applicability in dynamic environments where novel categories continuously emerge. To bridge this gap, we target the practical scenario of Federated Graph Generalized Category Discovery (FGGCD), aiming to collaboratively discover novel categories across decentralized graph clients while retaining knowledge of known categories. We observe that FGGCD introduces two fundamental challenges: (1) the Neighborhood Absorption Effect, where structural fragmentation leads to biased neighborhood aggregation, causing novel nodes to be misclassified as known categories; and (2) Global Semantic Inconsistency, where the aforementioned local biases propagate to the server and are amplified by heterogeneous subgraph distributions, hindering cross-client knowledge integration. To address these issues, we propose GCD-FGL, an FGL framework for GCD that integrates a client-side Topology-Reliable Semantic Alignment and Discovery process to mitigate the neighborhood absorption effect, and a server-side Hierarchical Prototype Alignment strategy to resolve global semantic inconsistency. Extensive experiments on five real-world graph datasets demonstrate that GCD-FGL consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving an average absolute gain of +4.86 in HRScore.

</details>


### [104] [Quantile Geometry Regularization for Distributional Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08182)
*Zhaofan Zhang,Minghao Yang,Rufeng Chen,Sihong Xie,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为RQIQN的轻量化Wasserstein分布鲁棒增强方法，通过在分位数回归中引入分布鲁棒优化，有效缓解了基于分位数的分布式强化学习中的分布退化问题，并在风险敏感导航和Atari游戏中取得了优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于分位数的分布式强化学习方法（如IQN）在引导目标分位数时可能产生扭曲或退化的分布估计，需要一种能直接修复分位数几何特性且不改变底层价值目标的轻量级增强方案。

Method: 将IQN损失重新解释为一组在采样当前分数上的局部经验分位数估计问题；对每个局部槽位使用Wasserstein分布鲁棒分位数估计公式进行鲁棒化，得到闭合形式的、依赖于分数（fraction）的Bellman目标修正项。该修正具有中位数反对称性（保持风险中性分位数平均）和单调性（扩大上下分位数间距，对抗分布坍缩）。

Result: 在风险敏感导航任务和Atari游戏中，RQIQN的性能超过了其他现有的基于分位数的分布式强化学习算法。

Conclusion: RQIQN通过Wasserstein分布鲁棒优化对分位数估计进行轻量化正则化，在不改变底层价值目标或需要额外样本集重建的前提下，有效解决了分位数退化问题，是一种可广泛适用的增强方案。

Abstract: Quantile-based distributional reinforcement learning methods learn return distributions through sampled quantile regression, but their bootstrapped target quantiles may induce distorted or degenerate distribution estimates. We propose Robust Quantile-based Implicit Quantile Networks (RQIQN), a lightweight Wasserstein distributionally robust enhancement boosted from a quantile estimation perspective. We first reinterpret a snapshot of IQN loss as a collection of local empirical quantile estimation problems over sampled current fractions. We then robustify each local slot with a Wasserstein distributionally robust quantile estimation formulation, yielding a closed-form, fraction-dependent correction to the Bellman target. This correction directly addresses distributional degeneration: its median antisymmetry preserves the risk-neutral quantile average, while its monotonicity enlarges upper-lower quantile gaps and counteracts collapsed distributional spread. RQIQN thus regularizes quantile geometry without changing the underlying value objective or requiring additional sample set reconstruction. Finally, we empirically show that the proposed RQIQN outperforms other existing quantile-based distributional reinforcement learning algorithms in risk-sensitive navigation and Atari games.

</details>


### [105] [Synergistic Simplex: Cooperative Runtime Assurance for Safety-Critical Autonomous Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08190)
*Ayoosh Bansal,Mikael Yeghiazaryan,Artyom Khachatryan,Tianyi Zhu,Hunmin Kim,Naira Hovakimyan,Lui Sha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Synergistic Simplex (SS)架构，通过允许安全监控器使用机器学习组件的输出，实现ML组件与安全监控器的双向集成，在保持形式化安全保证的同时提升系统性能，并应用于自动驾驶障碍物检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运行时保证(RTA)系统中，安全监控器性能有限是主要瓶颈；传统方法禁止安全监控器使用ML输出，限制了系统整体性能。

Method: 提出Synergistic Simplex (SS)架构，实现ML组件与安全监控器的双向集成；形式化推导了在允许安全监控器使用ML输出的条件下保持安全性的条件。

Result: 在自动驾驶障碍物检测任务中进行了设计、分析和评估，证明了SS架构在保持形式化安全保证的同时带来了性能提升。

Conclusion: SS架构通过打破传统RTA中禁止安全监控器使用ML输出的限制，实现了性能与安全性的更好权衡，为安全关键系统中ML组件的可靠部署提供了新方案。

Abstract: Autonomous systems increasingly rely on machine-learning (ML) components for safety-critical tasks such as perception and control in autonomous vehicles (AVs). While ML enables essential capabilities, it inevitably exhibits long-tail faults that make it unsuitable for safety-critical tasks. Runtime assurance (RTA) mitigates this issue by pairing ML components with verifiable safety monitors, e.g., Control Simplex and Perception Simplex architectures. However, the limited performance of safety monitors remains a major bottleneck.
  The Synergistic Simplex (SS) architecture improves system performance by enabling bidirectional integration between ML components and safety monitors while preserving formal safety guarantees. The key innovation here is allowing safety monitors to use ML outputs, which is typically prohibited in RTA systems. We formally derive conditions under which this integration preserves safety and demonstrate the performance benefits. We present the design, analysis, and evaluation of SS for AV obstacle detection.

</details>


### [106] [ExecuTorch -- A Unified PyTorch Solution to Run AI Models On-Device](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08195)
*Mergen Nachin,Digant Desai,Sicheng Stephen Jia,Chen Lai,Mengwei Liu,Jacob Szwejbka,Raziel Alvarez,RJ Ascani,Dave Bort,Manuel Candales,Andrew Caples,Yanan Cao,Zhengxu Chen,Soumith Chintala,Gregory Comer,Tanvir Islam,Songhao Jia,Tarun Karuturi,Jack Khuu,Abhinay Kukkadapu,Tugsbayasgalan Manlaibaatar,Andrew Or,Kimish Patel,Siddartha Pothapragada,Lucy Qiu,Supriya Rao,Orion Reblitz-Richardson,Max Ren,Scott Roy,Anthony Shoumikhin,Scott Wolchok,Guang Yang,Angela Yi,Martin Yuan,Hansong Zhang,Jack Zhang,Jerry Zhang,Shunting Zhang,C. Cagatay Bilgin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ExecuTorch 是一个统一的 PyTorch 原生边缘AI部署框架，支持从微控制器到复杂SoC的异构计算环境，实现机器学习模型的无缝部署，并保持PyTorch语义，支持量化优化和可插拔执行后端，连接研究与生产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备上的AI执行对低延迟和离线运行至关重要，但在异构硬件上部署模型仍存在碎片化问题，通常需要在PyTorch生态系统之外进行模型转换或完全重新实现。

Method: ExecuTorch 是一个统一的PyTorch原生部署框架，保持PyTorch语义同时允许自定义，支持量化等优化，并提供可插拔的执行后端，使研究者能够在PyTorch内部验证部署行为。

Result: ExecuTorch 能够从嵌入式微控制器扩展到复杂系统级芯片（SoC），支持可穿戴设备、智能手机到大型计算集群，实现跨异构环境的无缝模型部署。

Conclusion: ExecuTorch 弥合了研究与生产之间的差距，允许快速实验并让研究者完全在PyTorch生态内验证部署行为。

Abstract: Local execution of AI on edge devices is important for low latency and offline operation. However, deploying models on diverse hardware remains fragmented, often requiring model conversion or complete reimplementation outside the PyTorch ecosystem where the model was originally authored. We introduce ExecuTorch, a unified PyTorch-native deployment framework for edge AI. ExecuTorch enables seamless deployment of machine learning models across heterogeneous compute environments. It scales from embedded microcontrollers to complex system-on-chips (SoCs) with dedicated accelerators, powering devices ranging from wearables and smartphones to large compute clusters. ExecuTorch preserves PyTorch semantics while allowing customization, support for optimizations like quantization, and pluggable execution "backends". These features together enable fast experimentation, allowing researchers to validate deployment behavior entirely within PyTorch, bridging the gap between research and production.

</details>


### [107] [ReplaySCM: A Benchmark for Executable Causal Mechanism Induction from Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08197)
*Serafim Batzoglou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReplaySCM是一个包含1300个项目的基准测试，用于评估语言模型从有限干预证据中归纳可执行因果机制的能力。它通过重放行为（而非公式字符串）评分，并引入Ordered/Block-order/Hidden-order/Hidden-roots等设置及Alternative-SCM任务。前沿LLM能推断部分功能父结构，但隐藏顺序或根结构时重放性能急剧下降。支持审计阶梯（Original→Extra Worlds→CEx）可将局部前驱模式覆盖率从0.8949提升至1.0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果基准大多评估局部答案或图结构，缺乏对从有限干预证据中归纳完整可执行因果机制能力的系统评估。

Method: 构建1300个由潜在布尔结构因果模型生成的二元世界项目，要求系统输出受限布尔DSL中的机制映射；通过解析、合法性/无环性检查，并在训练和保留干预世界上重放来评分；引入四种结构信息暴露设置（Ordered/Block-order/Hidden-order/Hidden-roots）和Alternative-SCM任务；设计支持审计阶梯（Original→Extra Worlds→Counterexample Audit）。

Result: 前沿LLM能推断部分功能父结构，但隐藏顺序或根结构时保留集重放性能显著下降；审计阶梯将局部前驱模式覆盖率从0.8949提升至0.9815再到1.0；Ordered/Hidden-order差距在更强证据下仍然存在。

Conclusion: ReplaySCM通过评估从有限干预证据中的可执行重放泛化能力，补充了现有答案级因果推理和图发现基准，但不声称能唯一识别潜在SCM。

Abstract: Most causal benchmarks for language models score local answers or graph structure. We introduce ReplaySCM, a 1,300 item benchmark for executable causal mechanism induction from finite interventional evidence. Each item contains binary worlds generated by a latent fully observed acyclic Boolean structural causal model (SCM). A system must output a mechanism map in a restricted Boolean DSL; the submission is parsed, checked for legality and acyclicity, and replayed on training and held-out intervention worlds. Scoring uses replay behavior rather than formula strings, so syntactically different mechanisms receive credit when they behave correctly. ReplaySCM varies the structural information disclosed to the model through Ordered, Block-order, Hidden-order, and Hidden-roots settings, and includes Alternative-SCM tasks that supply a valid reference SCM and ask for a semantically distinct alternative that fits the training worlds, together with a separating intervention and witness. Frontier LLMs infer parts of the functional-parent structure, but held-out replay drops sharply when order or root structure is hidden. We also evaluate a matched support-audit ladder: Original, Extra Worlds, and Counterexample Audit (CEx), that raises mean local predecessor-pattern coverage from 0.8949 to 0.9815 to 1.0; under the audited searches, no discovered semantic alternative remains consistent with the training worlds. The Ordered/Hidden-order gap persists under this stronger evidence. ReplaySCM complements answer-level causal reasoning and graph-discovery benchmarks by evaluating executable replay generalization from finite interventional evidence, without claiming unique identification of the latent SCM.

</details>


### [108] [FairHealth: An Open-Source Python Library for Trustworthy Healthcare AI in Low-Resource Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08198)
*Farjana Yesmin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FairHealth是一个面向医疗AI的开源Python库，专注于低收入国家（如孟加拉国）场景，提供联邦学习、公平性审计、可解释性、低资源分类、公平资源分配和数据集加载六大模块，填补了现有工具在公平性、隐私保护、可解释性和全球南方数据集方面的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗AI工具包存在四个关键缺口：缺乏针对生物信号和临床表格数据的集成公平性审计；缺少兼容标准ML工作流的隐私保护联邦学习工具；缺乏针对低带宽临床决策支持的可解释性工具；没有覆盖全球南方医疗数据集。FairHealth旨在填补这些空白，特别关注低收入国家（如孟加拉国）的需求。

Method: FairHealth基于五项同行评审研究贡献，提供六个模块：fairhealth.federated（同态加密联邦学习）、fairhealth.fairness（交叉性公平性指标）、fairhealth.explain（混合模糊-SHAP可解释性）、fairhealth.lowresource（多语言登革热分诊）、fairhealth.equity（公平救灾资源分配）和fairhealth.datasets（公共数据集加载器）。所有数据集公开可用，无需机构数据使用协议。

Result: 开发了一个统一、模块化的开源Python库，可通过pip install fairhealth安装，代码托管在GitHub上。库涵盖了联邦学习、公平性审计、可解释性、低资源分类和公平资源分配等关键功能。

Conclusion: FairHealth为医疗AI在低收入国家的可信部署提供了首个综合性开源工具包，特别关注全球南方场景，为低资源环境下的公平、隐私保护和可解释机器学习提供了实践支持。

Abstract: We present FairHealth, an open-source Python library that provides a unified, modular framework for trustworthy machine learning in healthcare applications, with particular focus on low-resource and low-income country (LMIC) settings such as Bangladesh. FairHealth addresses four critical gaps in existing healthcare AI toolkits: (1) the absence of integrated fairness auditing for biosignals and clinical tabular data; (2) the lack of privacy-preserving federated learning tools compatible with standard ML workflows; (3) missing explainability tools tailored for low-bandwidth clinical decision support; and (4) no existing toolkit covering Global South healthcare datasets. Built from five peer-reviewed research contributions, FairHealth provides six modules covering federated learning with homomorphic encryption (fairhealth.federated), intersectional fairness metrics (fairhealth.fairness), hybrid fuzzy-SHAP explainability (fairhealth.explain), multilingual dengue triage (fairhealth.lowresource), equitable disaster aid allocation (fairhealth.equity), and public dataset loaders (fairhealth.datasets). All datasets used are publicly available without institutional data use agreements. FairHealth is installable via pip install fairhealth(PyPI: pypi.org/project/fairhealth/) and available at https://github.com/Farjana-Yesmin/fairhealth.

</details>


### [109] [Beyond Penalization: Diffusion-based Out-of-Distribution Detection and Selective Regularization in Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08202)
*Qingjun Wang,Hongtu Zhou,Hang Yu,Junqiao Zhao,Yanping Zhao,Chen Ye,Ziqiao Wang,Guang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DOSER利用扩散模型的单步去噪重构误差作为OOD指标，通过区分有益和有害的OOD动作并选择性正则化，在离线强化学习中取得了优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线RL方法对OOD动作的惩罚过于笼统，未能准确识别OOD动作，可能抑制有益的探索；现有区分OOD样本的方法依赖严格的分布假设，判别能力有限。

Method: 训练两个扩散模型分别捕捉行为策略和状态分布，使用单步去噪重构误差作为OOD指标；在策略优化中通过评估预测转移来区分有益和有害的OOD动作，选择性抑制风险动作并鼓励高潜力探索。理论上证明了DOSER是γ-压缩映射且存在唯一不动点，并给出了渐近性能保证。

Result: 在广泛的离线RL基准测试中，DOSER一致优于先前方法，尤其在次优数据集上表现突出。

Conclusion: DOSER通过基于扩散模型的选择性正则化框架，有效解决了离线RL中OOD动作的过估问题，同时避免抑制有益的探索行为。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning (RL) faces a critical challenge of overestimating the value of out-of-distribution (OOD) actions. Existing methods mitigate this issue by penalizing unseen samples, yet they fail to accurately identify OOD actions and may suppress beneficial exploration beyond the behavioral support. Although several methods have been proposed to differentiate OOD samples with distinct properties, they typically rely on restrictive assumptions about the data distribution and remain limited in discrimination ability. To address this problem, we propose DOSER (Diffusion-based OOD Detection and Selective Regularization), a novel framework that goes beyond uniform penalization. DOSER trains two diffusion models to capture the behavior policy and state distribution, using single-step denoising reconstruction error as a reliable OOD indicator. During policy optimization, it further distinguishes between beneficial and detrimental OOD actions by evaluating predicted transitions, selectively suppressing risky actions while encouraging exploration of high-potential ones. Theoretically, we prove that DOSER is a $γ$-contraction and therefore admits a unique fixed point with bounded value estimates. We further provide an asymptotic performance guarantee relative to the optimal policy under model approximation and OOD detection errors. Across extensive offline RL benchmarks, DOSER consistently attains superior performance to prior methods, especially on suboptimal datasets.

</details>


### [110] [LLMs with in-context learning for Algorithmic Theoretical Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08212)
*Anamaria Hell,Leander Thiele*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过将Claude（大语言模型）与Maple（计算机代数系统）相结合，应用于修改引力理论中的宇宙学扰动计算，验证了配备CAS运行时的前沿LLM在完成理论物理中算法任务方面的能力与局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理论物理中算法计算日益增多，虽然概念简单但耗时且易出差错，因此需要探究LLM结合计算机代数系统是否能可靠地执行这些任务。

Method: 将Claude（LLM）与Maple（CAS）接口连接，并为其提供足够丰富的上下文信息和工作示例，应用于修改引力理论的宇宙学扰动问题。

Result: 配备工作示例的前沿LLM能够解决大多数测试问题。

Conclusion: LLM与CAS结合的方法在理论物理算法计算中具有潜力，但需注意其典型失败模式并加以改进。

Abstract: There is an increasing number of algorithmic computations in theoretical physics. These, while conceptually simple, can nevertheless be time-consuming and contain subtleties that should not be overlooked. Given the recent improvement of Large Language Models (LLM), it is natural to investigate whether LLMs equipped with a computer algebra system (CAS) runtime and sufficiently informative context can reliably carry out these algorithmic tasks. In this work, we interface Claude with Maple, and apply this framework to cosmological perturbations in modified theories of gravity. We demonstrate the current capabilities of this approach, the typical failures, and how the same can be improved. We find that a frontier LLM supplied with worked examples is able to solve most test problems.

</details>


### [111] [Deep Dreams Are Made of This: Visualizing Monosemantic Features in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08218)
*Adam Szokalski,Mateusz Modrzejewski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LVO方法，通过稀疏自编码器解耦潜在扩散模型的多语义特征，结合潜在空间优化、时间步活动分析等策略，在Style50数据集上生成比基线更清晰的可视化结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将特征可视化优化技术从CNN扩展到潜在扩散模型，解决潜空间中多语义特征纠缠导致的解释性不足问题，为理解扩散模型内部表征提供机制性解释工具。

Method: 采用稀疏自编码器(SAE)解耦多语义层表征为单语义特征，结合潜在空间优化、时间步活动分析、调度匹配噪声注入、特征引导先验初始化和适当正则化策略，在Stable Diffusion 1.5（Style50微调版）上实现可视化。

Result: SAE特征生成的对角线构图、人物、玫瑰、电缆、瀑布泡沫等概念的可视化结果与数据集样本高度相关，而未经解耦的基线产生模糊结果；像素空间的正则化技术可迁移至潜在域但配置不同。

Conclusion: LVO通过直接揭示激活特征的内容（而非下游效应），为数据集示例和特征引导提供互补性洞察，是扩散模型机制解释性的有效工具。

Abstract: This paper proposes latent visualization by optimization (LVO), a mechanistic interpretability technique that extends feature visualization by optimization - originally developed for convolutional neural networks - to latent diffusion models. LVO employs sparse autoencoders (SAEs) to disentangle polysemantic layer representations into monosemantic features. Key contributions include latent-space optimization, time-step activity analysis, schedule-matched noise injection, prior initialization through feature steering, and suitable regularization strategies. We demonstrate the method on Stable Diffusion 1.5 fine-tuned on the Style50 dataset, showing that SAE features produce clear visualizations of recognizable concepts - including diagonal compositions, human figures, roses, cables, and waterfall foam - that correlate with dataset examples, while the baseline without disentanglement produces less coherent results. We further show that regularization techniques from pixel-space feature visualization transfer to the latent domain, though they require different configurations for the raw-layer and SAE variants. Compared to dataset examples and steering, LVO provides complementary insights by directly revealing what activates a feature rather than its downstream effects.

</details>


### [112] [NoisyCoconut: Counterfactual Consensus via Latent Space Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08221)
*Michael Jerge,David Evans*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NoisyCoconut是一种无需重新训练的推理时方法，通过对模型内部表示注入可控噪声生成多样化推理路径，利用路径间一致性作为置信度信号，使模型在不确定时选择性弃权，从而显著提升大语言模型可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提升LLM可靠性的方法依赖微调或训练数据，成本高昂且与已有模型兼容性差。本文旨在开发一种无需训练数据、无需修改模型参数、仅在推理时就能提升模型可靠性的实用方法。

Method: 在推理过程中，向模型的潜在轨迹中注入可控噪声，生成多条不同的推理路径。通过评估这些路径之间的一致性（如完全一致作为高置信度信号），使模型在路径不一致时选择弃权。

Result: 在多个推理基准上，该方法有效实现了覆盖率和准确率之间的权衡。噪声扰动路径的完全一致将错误率从40-70%降至15%以下，通过选择性弃权，在数学推理任务上准确率超过95%。

Conclusion: NoisyCoconut提供了一种实用的、无训练的推理时干预方法，能有效提升LLM输出的可靠性，且与现有模型完全兼容，为在实际应用中部署更可靠的LLM提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This paper presents NoisyCoconut, a novel inference-time method that enhances large language model (LLM) reliability by manipulating internal representations. Unlike fine-tuning methods that require extensive retraining, NoisyCoconut operates directly on model representations during inference and requires no retraining. Rather than training models to reason in latent space, we inject controlled noise into latent trajectories to generate diverse reasoning paths. Agreement among these paths provides a confidence signal, enabling models to abstain when uncertain. We demonstrate that this approach achieves effective coverage-accuracy tradeoffs across multiple reasoning benchmarks without requiring access to training data or modification of model parameters. This approach provides a practical pathway to improving the reliability of LLM outputs while maintaining compatibility with existing models. Our experiments show that unanimous agreement among noise-perturbed paths reduces error rates from 40-70% to below 15%, enabling models to exceed 95% accuracy on mathematical reasoning tasks through selective abstention.

</details>


### [113] [A Simulated Federated Analysis of MS-Induced Brain Lesions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08223)
*Evelyn Trautmann,Joël Federer-Gsponer,Markus C. Elze,José-Tomás Prieto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个模拟真实联邦学习项目的仿真框架，用于多发性硬化症（MS）患者数据分析，包含图像分割和临床数据分析两个任务，采用联邦生存分析和联邦PCA方法，并利用高保真合成队列和公开数据集来模拟真实临床数据的复杂性和异质性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习等联邦技术在保护患者隐私的同时实现多中心临床数据研究具有巨大潜力，但缺乏真实场景下的测试平台。本文旨在构建一个能够模拟真实联邦研究工作流的仿真框架，以支持MS研究中的联邦学习方法开发、评估和基准测试。

Method: 构建了一个包含两个组件的联邦研究项目：图像分割任务（使用公开真实数据集）和临床数据分析任务（采用联邦生存分析和联邦PCA）。通过构建高保真合成队列来模拟MS相关的临床和人口统计学特征，并复现了分布式数据治理、站点特异性预处理、隔离节点上的模型训练以及分析结果的安全聚合等关键联邦工作流要素。

Result: 成功构建了一个能够模拟真实联邦学习场景的仿真框架，为MS研究中的联邦学习方法提供了现实的测试平台。

Conclusion: 该仿真框架为开发、评估和基准测试MS研究中的联邦学习方法提供了一个逼真的测试环境，能够有效捕捉真实临床数据集的复杂性和异质性。

Abstract: Federated techniques such as federated learning and federated analysis have emerged as a powerful paradigm for enabling multi-center research on sensitive clinical data while preserving patient privacy. In this study, we introduce a simulation framework that emulates a real-world federated research project focused on the analysis of multiple sclerosis (MS) patient data. The project comprises two components: an image segmentation task and a clinical data analysis task, where federated variants of survival analysis and Principal Component Analysis (PCA) are employed. To capture the complexity and heterogeneity of real clinical datasets, we construct a federation of high-fidelity synthetic cohorts designed to mirror MS-related clinical and demographic characteristics, while the imaging component leverages publicly available real-world datasets. Our simulation replicates key elements of authentic federated workflows, including distributed data governance, site-specific preprocessing, model training across isolated nodes, and the secure aggregation of analytical outputs. This framework provides a realistic testbed for developing, evaluating, and benchmarking federated learning methods in the context of MS research.

</details>


### [114] [TRAM: Training Approximate Multiplier Structures for Low-Power AI Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08231)
*Chang Meng,Hanyu Wang,Yuyang Ye,Mingfei Yu,Wayne Burleson,Giovanni De Micheli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRAM是一种联合优化近似乘法器结构和AI模型参数的方法，相比现有技术，在CNN和视觉Transformer上分别实现了最高25.05%和27.09%的乘法器功耗降低，且精度损失很小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI加速器的功耗问题日益严重，而乘法器是AI模型中功耗最高的组件之一。现有近似乘法器设计通常与AI模型训练分离进行，未能充分利用模型参数与近似乘法器之间的协同优化潜力。

Method: 提出TRAM方法，将近似乘法器（AxM）的结构优化与AI模型的参数训练进行联合优化，在保持较小精度损失的前提下降低功耗。

Result: 在CIFAR-10上的CNN任务中，相比最先进的近似乘法器，TRAM实现了最高25.05%的乘法器功耗降低；在ImageNet上的视觉Transformer任务中，实现了最高27.09%的功耗降低。

Conclusion: TRAM通过联合优化AxM结构和AI模型参数，在显著降低功耗的同时仅带来很小的精度损失，优于独立设计AxM的现有方法。

Abstract: Reducing power consumption in AI accelerators is increasingly important. Approximate computing can reduce power consumption while keeping the accuracy loss small. Since multipliers are power-hungry components in AI models, this paper focuses on synthesizing low-power approximate multipliers (AxMs). Unlike prior works that design AxMs separately from AI model training, we present TRAM, which jointly optimizes the AxM structure and AI model parameters to lower power with small accuracy loss. Experiments show that compared to state-of-the-art AxMs, TRAM achieves up to 25.05% AxM power reduction on CNNs with CIFAR-10, and reduces power by up to 27.09% on vision transformers with ImageNet.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [115] [UserGPT Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08766)
*Yunyi Xuan,Hao Yi,Fengling Mao,Daye Cai,Leikun Liang,Xingsheng He,Jiangnan Xie,Guoshuai Wang,Yushan Han,Wenwen Guo,Xiaoxiao Xu,Lin Qu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出UserGPT框架，利用大语言模型从大规模数字行为轨迹中生成连贯的用户画像摘要，并构建了用户行为模拟引擎、数据语义化模块和课程式后训练策略（含DF-GRPO），在自建基准HPR-Bench上实现了标签预测Acc@10=0.7325和摘要生成Acc_Ex=0.7528，行为记录压缩率达97.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统用户画像方法依赖判别式模型和手工特征工程，预测离散属性时往往产生碎片化、逻辑不一致的画像，且对长尾行为泛化能力差。本文旨在探索生成式范式下，利用LLM将长序列噪声行为历史总结为连贯叙事，以实现细粒度的用户演化理解。

Method: 提出UserGPT框架：(1) 用户行为模拟引擎生成真实复杂轨迹；(2) 数据为中心的语义化模块将异构行为日志转化为结构化语义输入；(3) 课程式后训练策略结合多阶段SFT与双过滤组相对策略优化(DF-GRPO)增强长历史推理能力。

Result: 在HPR-Bench上，UserGPT在标签预测上达到Avg@10=0.7325，摘要生成上达到Acc_Ex=0.7528，行为记录压缩率高达97.9%且关键信息保留。

Conclusion: UserGPT在全景式人物推理和个性化用户-智能体交互中表现有效，证明了生成式模型在用户理解任务中的潜力。

Abstract: Personalized user understanding from large-scale digital traces remains a fundamental challenge. Traditional user profiling methods rely on discriminative models and manual feature engineering to predict discrete attributes, often producing fragmented and logically inconsistent profiles that generalize poorly to long-tail behaviors. In this work, we study a generative paradigm in which large language models (LLMs) summarize long and noisy behavioral histories into coherent narratives that capture nuanced user evolution. Our experiments show that even strong LLMs remain limited in complex and implicit personalization reasoning.
  We propose UserGPT, a framework for improving LLM-based persona understanding through both attribute generation and summary generation. To address the scarcity of real-world behavioral data, we develop a User Behavior Simulation Engine that produces realistic and complex user trajectories. We further introduce a Data-Centric Semantization module that transforms heterogeneous behavioral logs into structured and semantically coherent inputs, reducing noise and sparsity. On top of this pipeline, we design a curriculum-driven post-training strategy that combines multi-stage Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) with Dual-Filter Group Relative Policy Optimization (DF-GRPO) to strengthen reasoning over long behavioral histories.
  We also construct HPR-Bench, a benchmark for holistic persona reasoning derived from simulated data. On HPR-Bench, UserGPT achieves an Avg@10 score of 0.7325 on tag prediction and an $Acc_{Ex}$ score of 0.7528 on summary generation, while compressing behavioral records by up to 97.9% with critical information preserved. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of UserGPT for holistic persona reasoning and personalized user-agent interaction.

</details>


### [116] [Reddit2Deezer: A Scalable Dataset for Real-World Grounded Conversational Music Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09120)
*Haven Kim,Julian McAuley*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了Reddit2Deezer，一个基于Reddit真实对话构建的音乐推荐资源，包含19万条{主题, 叶评论}对，提供原始版和转述版两个版本，且所有音乐实体均链接到Deezer标识符以便获取音频预览和元数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话式音乐推荐研究面临两难：真实对话语料规模有限，而合成语料虽然可扩展但对话是人工构造而非自然观察到的。

Method: 从Reddit提取19万条{主题, 叶评论}对，构建原始版（保持真实性）和转述版（最大化长期可复现性），并将每个音乐实体链接到Deezer标识符以获取音频预览和丰富元数据（如类型标签、流行度、BPM）。

Result: 通过人类验证确认了对话质量、项目锚定和转述质量。数据集已发布在Hugging Face上。

Conclusion: Reddit2Deezer为内容驱动的对话式推荐研究提供了真实、可扩展且可复现的资源，解决了真实语料与合成语料之间的权衡问题。

Abstract: Conversational music recommendation (CMR) research currently faces a tradeoff between authentic dialogue corpora that are limited in scale and synthesized corpora that scale up but whose conversations are artificially constructed rather than naturally observed. In this paper, we introduce Reddit2Deezer, a reality-grounded CMR resource derived from 190k unique {thread, leaf-comment} pairs. We release the resource in two versions: a raw version that preserves authenticity, and a paraphrased version that maximizes long-term reproducibility. Each musical entity is linked to a Deezer identifier, which provides straightforward access to audio previews and rich metadata (e.g., genre tags, popularity, BPM), opening the door to future research on content-grounded conversational recommendation. A human validation confirms the quality of the dialogues, item grounding, and paraphrases. The dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/McAuley-Lab/Reddit2Deezer.

</details>


### [117] [OpenIIR: An Open Simulation Platform for Information Retrieval Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09321)
*Saber Zerhoudi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: OpenIIR是一个可复现的多智能体IR研究框架，支持四种实验类型（审议小组、社交媒体、策划推送、多代演化），通过参数化配置和结构化输出来模拟LLM驱动的人设行为，并提供可插拔的场景接口和共享核心组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的信息检索(IR)研究缺乏可复现、参数化的多智能体实验平台，研究者难以系统比较不同配置（如人设预算、检索策略、排序器选择、干预时机等）对IR系统行为的影响。

Method: 构建共享核心（智能体运行时、世界模型存储、检索原语、声明提取器、人设本体），定义可插拔场景的类型接口，实现四种参考研究类型（Panel、Social-Media、Curated-Feed、Multi-Generational），每个新研究以200-400行插件形式集成。

Result: 发布了四种参考研究的可复现运行结果，每种生成结构化输出（论证图、曝光日志、适应度追踪、对话记录），并针对开放IR研究问题提供了六种模块化扩展方案的草图。

Conclusion: OpenIIR提供了一个标准化、可复现的多智能体IR实验框架，使研究者能够以前向声明参数的方式配置实验并直接比较不同设置下的结果，填补了现有工具的空白。

Abstract: OpenIIR runs hundreds of LLM-driven personas as parameterised, reproducible IR research experiments. Researchers configure agents across four kinds of multi-agent study (deliberative panels, social platforms, curated recommender feeds, and evolutionary co-evolution between content producers and credibility detectors) under many priors, rounds, and constraints. Persona budgets, retrieval policies, ranker choices, intervention timings, and mutation rates are declared up front, and the same study can be re-run under different settings to compare outcomes side by side. Every run produces structured outputs (argument graphs, exposure logs, fitness traces, transcripts) that a downstream evaluator can consume directly, and a new study is a 200--400 line plug-in over a shared core (agent runtime, world-model store, retrieval primitives, claim extractor, persona ontology). The contributions are: (i) the shared core; (ii) a type interface for pluggable scenarios; (iii) four released types with reference runs (Panel, Social-Media, Curated-Feed, Multi-Generational); and (iv) six modular extensions sketched against open IR research questions.

</details>


### [118] [A General Framework for Multimodal LLM-Based Multimedia Understanding in Large-Scale Recommendation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09338)
*Yiming Zhu,Xu Liu,Ziyun Xu,Zheng Wu,Joena Zhang,Sirius Chen,Chenheli Hua,Silvester Yao,Qichao Que,Wentao Shi,Junfeng Pan,Linhong Zhu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于多模态大语言模型(MM-LLM)的三元架构框架，通过LLaMA2生成描述性字幕并转化为分类型的token特征，在工业级推荐系统中实现了离线AUC提升0.35%和在线指标提升0.02%，证明了MM-LLM在大规模推荐场景中的实用可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推荐系统未能充分利用多媒体内容中的高维语义信号，限制了用户偏好建模的准确性。尽管MM-LLM能理解复杂数据，但将其集成到延迟敏感的工业级架构中仍面临巨大挑战。

Method: 提出三元架构：(1)内容解释(Content Interpretation)；(2)表示提取(Representation Extraction)；(3)系统化流水线集成(Systematic Pipeline Integration)。具体采用LLaMA2模型生成描述性字幕，并将其作为tokenized分类特征输入推荐系统。

Result: 离线AUC提升0.35%，在线指标提升0.02%，验证了该方法在大规模场景下的实际有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了利用MM-LLM增强大规模推荐系统性能的实践可行性，为多媒体理解与推荐系统的高效融合提供了通用框架。

Abstract: Conventional recommendation systems frequently fail to fully exploit the high-dimensional semantic signals inherent in multimedia content, thereby limiting the fidelity of user preference modeling. While Multimodal Large Language Models (MM-LLMs) offer robust mechanisms for interpreting such complex data, their integration into latency-constrained, industrial-scale architectures remains a significant challenge. To address this, we propose a generalized framework for MM-LLM-driven multimedia understanding. Our methodology employs a tripartite architecture encompassing content interpretation, representation extraction, and systematic pipeline integration, instantiated via a LLaMA2-based model that generates descriptive captions subsequently ingested as tokenized categorical features. Empirical evaluation demonstrates the efficacy of this approach, yielding a $0.35\%$ increase in offline AUC and a $0.02\%$ improvement in online metrics at scale, substantiating the practical viability of leveraging MM-LLMs to enhance large-scale recommendation performance.

</details>


### [119] [Loom: Hybrid Retrieval-Scoring Outfit Recommendation with Semantic Material Compatibility and Occasion-Aware Embedding Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09830)
*Anushree Berlia*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Loom是一个结合神经嵌入检索与结构化领域评分的穿搭推荐系统，通过FashionCLIP嵌入的槽约束近似近邻搜索检索互补单品，并利用六信号多目标函数评分。提出了语义材质权重和氛围/反氛围场合先验两种技术，在620件商品目录上实现0.179平均评分和9.3%硬违规率，相比随机基线提升3.3倍评分并减少42%违规，方向重排序是最关键组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有纯学习或纯规则方法在穿搭推荐中存在局限性，需要一种既能利用语义嵌入能力又能融入结构化领域知识的方法来生成完整、连贯的穿搭方案。

Method: (1) 基于FashionCLIP嵌入的槽约束近似近邻搜索检索互补单品；(2) 六信号多目标评分函数（嵌入相似度、色彩和谐度、风格一致性、场合连贯性、风格方向、穿搭内多样性）；(3) 语义材质权重，利用CLIP嵌入几何推断衣物厚重程度；(4) 氛围/反氛围场合先验，将场合描述嵌入CLIP空间作为锚向量。

Result: 全系统在620件商品目录上平均评分0.179，硬违规率9.3%，相比类别约束随机基线（评分0.054，违规率16.0%）评分提升3.3倍，违规减少42%。方向重排序是最关键组件，移除后评分降至0.052。系统可在普通硬件上5秒内生成三种风格不同的穿搭。

Conclusion: Loom验证了神经嵌入检索与结构化评分相结合在穿搭推荐中的有效性，语义材质权重和氛围先验两种技术有效弥补了纯学习或纯规则方法的不足，方向重排序是保证系统性能的核心组件。

Abstract: We present Loom, an outfit recommendation system that combines neural embedding retrieval with structured domain scoring to generate complete, coherent outfits from fashion catalogs. Given an anchor clothing item, Loom retrieves complementary pieces via slot-constrained approximate nearest neighbor search over FashionCLIP embeddings, then scores candidate outfits using a multi-objective function that integrates six signals: embedding similarity, color harmony, formality consistency, occasion coherence, style direction, and within-outfit diversity.
  We introduce two techniques that address limitations of purely learned or purely rule-based approaches: (1) semantic material weight, which uses CLIP embedding geometry to infer garment heaviness for layer compatibility without hand-coded material taxonomies; and (2) vibe/anti-vibe occasion priors, which embed prose descriptions of occasion contexts as anchor vectors in CLIP space and score items by differential affinity.
  Ablation experiments on a catalog of 620 items show that each component contributes measurably to outfit quality: the full system achieves a mean outfit score of 0.179 with a 9.3% hard violation rate, compared to 0.054 score and 16.0% violations for a category-constrained random baseline, a 3.3x improvement in score and 42% reduction in violations. Direction reranking is the single indispensable component: removing it drops score to 0.052, essentially equal to random. The system generates three stylistically distinct outfits in under 5 seconds on commodity hardware.

</details>


### [120] [ReCoVR: Closing the Loop in Interactive Composed Video Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09836)
*Bingqing Zhang,Yi Zhang,Zhuo Cao,Yang Li,Xue Li,Jiajun Liu,Sen Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ReCoVR 通过双通道反射感知架构，将检索历史作为诊断证据，解决了交互式组合视频检索中多轮渐进式搜索的结构性缺陷，在 WebVid-CoVR-Test 上仅一轮交互即达 74.30% R@1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有组合视频检索（CoVR）仅支持单轮交互，无法满足现实世界中用户通过多轮自然语言反馈逐步细化搜索意图的渐进式需求。

Method: 提出 ReCoVR（反射式组合视频检索），包含双通道架构：意图通道（Intent Pathway）将异构反馈路由到互补检索通道，反射通道（Reflection Pathway）执行轨迹级反思，监测结果演化并跨轮纠正检索错误。

Result: 在多个基准上一致优于交互式基线，在 WebVid-CoVR-Test 数据集上仅一轮交互即达到 74.30% R@1。

Conclusion: 将检索历史作为诊断证据的双通道反射感知架构，能够有效解决交互式检索中的轨迹漂移和停滞问题，显著提升多轮组合视频检索性能。

Abstract: Composed video retrieval (CoVR) searches for target videos using a reference video and a modification text, but existing methods are restricted to a single interaction round and cannot support the progressive nature of real-world visual search. To bridge this gap, we first formalize interactive composed video retrieval, a multi-turn extension of CoVR, where users progressively refine their search intent through natural-language feedback across turns. Adapting existing interactive retrieval methods to this setting reveals two structural weaknesses: reliance on a single retrieval channel and an open-loop retrieval design that consumes user feedback but does not diagnose whether its own retrieval trajectory is drifting or stagnating. To address these limitations, we propose ReCoVR (Reflexive Composed Video Retrieval), a dual-pathway architecture built on reflexive perception, where the system treats its retrieval history as diagnostic evidence alongside user feedback. Specifically, an Intent Pathway routes heterogeneous feedback to complementary retrieval channels, while a Reflection Pathway performs trajectory-level reflection to monitor result evolution and correct retrieval errors across turns. Experiments on multiple benchmarks show that ReCoVR consistently outperforms interactive baselines, notably achieving 74.30% R@1 after just one interactive round on the WebVid-CoVR-Test dataset.

</details>


### [121] [OpenZL: Using Graphs to Compress Smaller and Faster](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09928)
*Yann Collet,Nick Terrell,W. Felix Handte,Danielle Rozenblit,Victor Zhang,Kevin Zhang,Yaelle Goldschlag,Jennifer Lee,Elliot Gorokhovsky,Yonatan Komornik,Daniel Riegel,Stan Angelov,Nadav Rotem*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出OpenZL，一种基于图模型的新型压缩框架，将压缩表示为由模块化编解码器组成的有向无环图，支持快速开发应用专用压缩器，在压缩率、速度和可维护性上均优于通用压缩器和深度学习压缩器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有压缩技术存在两难困境：通用压缩器性能有限，而应用专用压缩器虽性能优越但开发困难、维护成本高、适用范围窄。工业界需要一种兼具高性能、高吞吐、低资源消耗且易于开发和维护的压缩方案。

Method: 提出"图模型"压缩理论框架，将压缩过程建模为模块化编解码器的有向无环图（DAG）。OpenZL实现该框架，数据被压缩成自描述的线格式，可通过通用解码器解压任意配置。通过复用模块化编解码器，实现应用专用压缩器的快速开发。

Result: 在多种真实数据集上，OpenZL在压缩率和速度上均优于最先进的通用压缩器；与聚焦压缩比的深度学习压缩器相比，压缩率相当但速度快数个数量级；在Meta内部部署显示大小和/或速度持续改进，开发周期从数月缩短至数天。

Conclusion: OpenZL代表了实用、可扩展、可维护的数据压缩领域的重大进步，为现代数据密集型应用提供了兼顾压缩性能与工程效率的新范式。
